 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	---
     2	name: verify-fs-billing
     3	description: Launch and drive FS-Billing (React web portal + admin shell in apps/web, Fastify API with Better Auth in apps/api, Postgres) the way a user does, on an isolated throwaway stack, and capture proof (screenshots, ARIA snapshots, HTTP transcripts, SQL rows). Use to prove login, lockout, session expiry, logout, tenant isolation, theme/mobile, and 404 behavior end to end, or to check a change in the real app instead of unit tests. Also says how to run the separate FreeSWITCH/SIPp e2e surface.
     4	---
     5	
     6	# Verify FS-Billing
     7	
     8	FS-Billing is a call-billing system. Its user surface is the web app: a customer portal (`/`, `/login`) and an admin shell (`/admin`, `/admin/login`). Both are served by `apps/web` (Vite + React) and call `apps/api` (Fastify + Better Auth) over `/api`. Postgres stores business rows under RLS and the `auth.*` schema. Secondary surfaces:
     9	
    10	- **HTTP API.** Driven with `scripts/api.sh`. The contract is `packages/shared/src/api/contract.ts`.
    11	- **Auth CLIs.** `auth:seed`, `auth:unlock`, and `auth:bootstrap` in `apps/api` (`docs/phase-4-api.md`). Run them through `scripts/with-env.sh`.
    12	- **FreeSWITCH/SIPp e2e.** `freeswitch/test/run-integration.sh`. It has no UI and the browser harness does not drive it. See [features/freeswitch-sipp.md](features/freeswitch-sipp.md).
    13	- **Worker** (`apps/worker`). Not started by this skill. The SIPp e2e covers it.
    14	
    15	The feature map in [features/README.md](features/README.md) is the source for what to verify and how. Read it before you drive anything.
    16	
    17	All commands run from the repo root, with `S=.cursor/skills/verify-fs-billing/scripts`.
    18	
    19	## Isolation rules (read first)
    20	
    21	- The user's dev Postgres container `fs-billing-postgres-1` runs on `127.0.0.1:5432`. **Never touch it or its databases.** `sql.sh` and `cleanup.sh` refuse to act on it, and every helper acts only on the container labelled `fs-billing-verify=<RUN_ID>`.
    22	- The root `.env` points at `:5432`, and every pnpm script loads it with `--env-file-if-exists`. Node never lets a `.env` value override a variable that is already exported. `launch.sh` and `with-env.sh` export every DB, auth, and port variable, so repo commands reach the run's container. Never run `pnpm db:*`, `auth:*`, or `test:*` bare in this skill. Always prefix them with `$S/with-env.sh`.
    23	- Do not use `apps/web/e2e` (`pnpm test:e2e`) for verification. It hard-codes ports 3000/4173 and reuses whatever server is already there.
    24	- Every run gets its own container, free ports, secrets, and evidence dir, so two runs can coexist. The SIPp e2e is the exception: it uses fixed host-network ports.
    25	
    26	## Launch
    27	
    28	```sh
    29	S=.cursor/skills/verify-fs-billing/scripts
    30	$S/launch.sh            # about 10 s; the last line printed is the RUN_ID
    31	```
    32	
    33	From an empty database, `launch.sh` does the following:
    34	
    35	1. It picks a `RUN_ID` (`YYYYMMDDTHHMMSSZ-xxxxxx`) and creates the state dir `/tmp/verify-fs-billing/<RUN_ID>/` (mode 700). It records the run as current in `/tmp/verify-fs-billing/current`.
    36	2. It writes throwaway secrets (owner/app/auth DB passwords, `BETTER_AUTH_SECRET`, `SEED_AUTH_PASSWORD`) to `run.env` (mode 600). It never prints them.
    37	3. It starts `docker run postgres:17.9-alpine` as `fsb-verify-<RUN_ID>`, with label `fs-billing-verify=<RUN_ID>`, a free `127.0.0.1` port, and tmpfs data. It waits until TCP `pg_isready` answers, which happens only after image init finishes.
    38	4. It runs migrate, seed, and migrate again to check idempotence (`packages/db/src/bin/*.ts` via `tsx`), then `apps/api/scripts/seed-auth.ts` (`auth:seed`). Every seeded account gets `SEED_AUTH_PASSWORD`.
    39	5. It starts the API (`node --import tsx src/main.ts`) under `setsid` on a free port. `BETTER_AUTH_URL` is set to the API origin, and `BETTER_AUTH_TRUSTED_ORIGINS` to the API and web origins.
    40	6. It runs `vite build --outDir <state>/web-dist`, so `apps/web/dist` is never touched. Then it starts `vite preview --strictPort` under `setsid` on a free port, with `FS_BILLING_API_ORIGIN` set so `/api` proxies to this run's API.
    41	7. **Ready** means a real `POST /api/auth/login` as `bjh003@example.com` through the web origin returns 200 with `"kind":"customer"`, followed by a logout. The transcript is saved to `docs/evidence/verify-fs-billing/<RUN_ID>/launch/ready-login.http`.
    42	
    43	It prints `READY` with the web, API, and Postgres addresses. If it fails, it prints the cleanup command to run for that `RUN_ID`. Prerequisites: Docker, Node 22/24, pnpm 10.34.7, `pnpm install --frozen-lockfile` already done, Playwright Chromium (`pnpm --filter @fs-billing/web exec playwright install chromium` if missing), `ss`, and `python3`.
    44	
    45	To work on an older run, set `VERIFY_RUN_ID=<id>` in front of any helper. Otherwise the helpers use the run in `/tmp/verify-fs-billing/current`.
    46	
    47	## Doctor
    48	
    49	```sh
    50	$S/doctor.sh            # exit 0 = healthy, 1 = at least one FAIL line
    51	```
    52	
    53	Doctor prints one `OK` or `FAIL` line per check:
    54	
    55	- The container is running and is ours: id, label, and port all match.
    56	- The API and web PIDs are alive and carry `VERIFY_RUN_ID=<RUN_ID>` in `/proc/<pid>/environ`. The listener on each port belongs to that process group.
    57	- The git HEAD equals the revision recorded at launch. If HEAD moved, relaunch.
    58	- Applied migrations equal the entries in `packages/db/migrations/meta/_journal.json`. Tenants and credentials exist.
    59	- `/api/health` returns `{"status":"ok","service":"api"}`.
    60	- The web serves the built `index.html`, and `/api` proxies to our API.
    61	- A seeded login as `bjh003@example.com` works.
    62	
    63	The login is the only write: one session row, which is deleted again at once. No journey uses `bjh003`. Run doctor first, and again whenever something looks wrong. Do not drive a run that fails doctor.
    64	
    65	## Drive
    66	
    67	The browser harness is Playwright (`@playwright/test` 1.64 from `apps/web` devDependencies). `drive.mjs` resolves it from there:
    68	
    69	```sh
    70	$S/drive.mjs customer-login          # or: admin-login login-errors session-expiry cross-tenant logout theme-and-mobile not-found
    71	$S/drive.mjs customer-login --headed # watch it
    72	```
    73	
    74	Each journey runs headless Chromium (`locale zh-CN`, `Asia/Shanghai`) against the run's web origin. It prints a step table and exits `0` on PASS, `1` on FAIL, or `2` on BLOCKED (precondition missing; the reason is printed). Journeys use only ARIA roles and names taken from `apps/web/src`:
    75	
    76	| UI | Handle |
    77	| --- | --- |
    78	| Login form | `getByLabel('登录邮箱')`, `getByLabel('密码', { exact: true })`, `getByRole('button', { name: '登录', exact: true })` |
    79	| Login headings | portal `heading '登录'`, admin `heading '登录管理后台'`; admin page also shows `客户请使用客户门户登录` |
    80	| Login problems | `getByRole('alert')`: `登录邮箱或密码错误` / `请检查后重新输入`; `登录失败次数过多，已暂时锁定` + `请在 M分SS秒 后重试` (also the disabled submit button's name); `该账号已停用` / `如需恢复使用，请联系客服` |
    81	| Expired banner | `getByRole('status')`: `登录已过期，请重新登录` · `登录后将返回之前的页面` (on `?reason=expired`) |
    82	| Expired dialog | `getByRole('dialog', { name: '登录已过期，请重新登录' })` → `button '重新登录'` |
    83	| Portal home | `heading '首页'`, crumb text `ABC001 · 深圳市某某科技有限公司`, `getByRole('region', { name: '当前余额' })` → `-¥35.20` |
    84	| Admin home | `heading '概览'`, text `欢迎，王敏。…`, `html[data-product=admin]` |
    85	| Shell (desktop) | `button '主题'` (◐) → `menuitemradio '浅色' / '深色' / '跟随系统'`; `button '账号菜单'` → `menu` → `menuitem '退出登录'`; `navigation '主导航'` |
    86	| Shell (mobile < 768px) | `button '打开导航'` → `dialog '导航'`; `button '账号菜单'` → `dialog '账号菜单'` with `radiogroup '主题'` and `button '退出登录'` |
    87	| 404 | `heading '找不到这个页面'`, `link '返回首页'`; admin, in the shell: `返回首页即回到「概览」` |
    88	
    89	Theme state is `html[data-theme]` plus `localStorage['fs-billing.theme']`. The zone is `html[data-product]`.
    90	
    91	HTTP goes through the web origin's `/api` proxy, the same path the browser uses. `api.sh` sends `Origin: <web origin>`, which the API requires on POST:
    92	
    93	```sh
    94	$S/api.sh login abc001@example.com a            # seeded password, cookie jar "a"
    95	$S/api.sh login abc001@example.com x --wrong    # fixed wrong password
    96	$S/api.sh GET /api/portal/context a
    97	$S/api.sh POST /api/auth/logout a
    98	$S/api.sh POST /api/admin/portal-accounts/<uuid>/reset-password a
    99	VERIFY_API_BASE=api $S/api.sh GET /api/health  # hit the API origin directly
   100	```
   101	
   102	Database access always goes into OUR container through `docker exec`, as the owner role:
   103	
   104	```sh
   105	$S/sql.sh "SELECT email_key, failed_count, locked_until FROM auth.login_failures"
   106	$S/sql.sh < some.sql
   107	```
   108	
   109	Repo CLIs run with the run's environment through `with-env.sh`, which also redacts their output:
   110	
   111	```sh
   112	$S/with-env.sh pnpm --filter @fs-billing/api auth:unlock hzx014@example.com   # emergency unlock
   113	$S/with-env.sh pnpm test:idor                                                # IDOR suite, scratch fs_billing_test DB in our container
   114	$S/with-env.sh pnpm --filter @fs-billing/api auth:bootstrap --kind portal --profile <portal-profile-uuid> --replace < /path/to/password-file
   115	```
   116	
   117	`auth:bootstrap --generate` prints a new password once, and `with-env.sh` cannot redact a value it does not know. If you use `--generate`, redirect its output into the state dir, not into evidence.
   118	
   119	Seeded accounts (from `packages/db/src/seed.ts`; all use `SEED_AUTH_PASSWORD`):
   120	
   121	| Account | Role in journeys |
   122	| --- | --- |
   123	| `abc001@example.com` | ABC001, CNY, balance −35.20: main customer |
   124	| `glb009@example.com` | GLB009, USD, −15.02: second tenant (B) |
   125	| `hzx014@example.com` | HZX014: lockout target |
   126	| `cdt018@example.com` | CDT018: tenant `disabled` |
   127	| `wangmin@example.com` | admin 王敏 |
   128	| `lijun@example.com` | admin 李俊: session revocation |
   129	| `bjh003@example.com` | reserved for launch and doctor |
   130	
   131	## Evidence
   132	
   133	Evidence goes to `docs/evidence/verify-fs-billing/<RUN_ID>/<feature-id>/`. Cleanup never deletes it.
   134	
   135	- `steps.md` and `result.json`: each step's action, PASS/FAIL/BLOCKED, and the observed detail.
   136	- `NN-name.png` (full-page screenshot) and `NN-name.aria.yml` (Playwright `ariaSnapshot`) before and after each user action.
   137	- `http.log`: every `/api/*` response the browser saw, with the request body (password `[REDACTED]`), status, and JSON body. `NN-name.http` holds the curl transcripts from `api.sh`.
   138	- `NN-name.sql.txt`: the query and its rows from our container. `NN-name.cli.txt`: CLI commands and their output.
   139	- `launch/ready-login.http` and, after cleanup, `logs/` (api, web, build, db-setup, and auth-seed logs, redacted) plus `revision.txt`.
   140	
   141	Every file written into evidence passes through redaction: secret values from `run.env` and `session_token=` cookie values become `[REDACTED]`. Never paste `run.env` or a raw cookie into evidence by hand. To check for leaks: `grep -rF -e "$(grep ^SEED_AUTH_PASSWORD= /tmp/verify-fs-billing/<RUN_ID>/run.env | cut -d= -f2)" docs/evidence/verify-fs-billing/<RUN_ID>` should print nothing.
   142	
   143	Proof standards:
   144	
   145	- **Real user path.** Use the login form, menus, and links in the built app, through the same `/api` proxy. No test-only endpoints, internal setters, or seeded cookies. The API has no test hooks, and the raw Better Auth router is not mounted.
   146	- **Action and resulting state.** Capture the screen before and after, plus the HTTP status and error `code` behind what the UI shows.
   147	- **Side effects in rows.** Check `auth.session` (created on login, deleted on logout and revocation, none kept for disabled accounts), `auth.login_failures` (`failed_count`, `locked_until`, deleted by unlock or a successful login), `auth.account.updated_at` (unchanged by a forbidden reset), `operation_logs`, and `tenant_balances`.
   148	- **DB edits stand in for elapsed time or an operator action.** Setting `auth.session.expires_at` into the past stands in for seven days passing. Deleting session rows is what an admin reset or `--replace` bootstrap does. Each such edit is saved as its own `.sql.txt`, and every journey says so. Never edit DB rows to make an assertion pass.
   149	- **Mocks only at real production boundaries.** This skill mocks nothing. React Query's tab-refocus refetch is triggered with a `visibilitychange` event, the only synthetic browser event in use. It stands in for the user switching tabs, which headless Chromium cannot do.
   150	- **Dry runs.** None of these flows has a dry-run mode. `auth:unlock` really deletes the counter row, and `auth:bootstrap --replace` really revokes sessions. Before you trust any `--dry-run` or "safe" mode added later, prove what it skips with `sql.sh` before and after.
   151	- **Blocked is not passed.** If a precondition is missing (an account locked, seed changed), the journey exits 2 with the reason. Report it as BLOCKED, and never edit the journey to get around it.
   152	
   153	## Cleanup
   154	
   155	```sh
   156	$S/cleanup.sh                           # current run
   157	VERIFY_RUN_ID=<id> $S/cleanup.sh        # a specific run
   158	```
   159	
   160	`cleanup.sh` does the following:
   161	
   162	- It sends TERM, then KILL after 5 s, to the API and web process groups recorded in `api.pid` and `web.pid`. It does this only while each PID still carries this run's `VERIFY_RUN_ID`. It never kills by name or port.
   163	- It runs `docker rm -f` on the recorded container id, and only if the id carries label `fs-billing-verify=<RUN_ID>`.
   164	- It copies redacted logs to `<evidence>/logs/`, then deletes `/tmp/verify-fs-billing/<RUN_ID>/` (secrets, cookie jars, web build).
   165	- It prints `evidence kept: <dir> (<n> files)` and exits non-zero if the evidence dir is missing.
   166	
   167	Run cleanup after every run, including failed launches. Confirm with `ls docs/evidence/verify-fs-billing/<RUN_ID>/` and `docker ps --filter label=fs-billing-verify`.
   168	
   169	Evidence under `docs/evidence/` is not gitignored. Commit it only when a PR needs the proof.
   170	
   171	## Helpers
   172	
   173	| Script | Invocation | Purpose |
   174	| --- | --- | --- |
   175	| `launch.sh` | `$S/launch.sh` | Isolated stack from an empty DB; prints RUN_ID |
   176	| `doctor.sh` | `$S/doctor.sh` | Read-only health and ownership check |
   177	| `drive.mjs` | `$S/drive.mjs <feature> [--headed]` | Playwright journey and evidence for one feature |
   178	| `api.sh` | `$S/api.sh login <email> <jar> [--wrong]` / `$S/api.sh <METHOD> <PATH> [jar] [json]` | Redacted HTTP transcript via the web proxy |
   179	| `sql.sh` | `$S/sql.sh "<sql>"` / `$S/sql.sh < file.sql` | psql as owner in OUR container only |
   180	| `with-env.sh` | `$S/with-env.sh <command...>` | Repo CLI with the run's env, output redacted |
   181	| `cleanup.sh` | `$S/cleanup.sh` | Kill our process groups, remove our container, keep evidence |
   182	| `lib.sh` | sourced | Run resolution, redaction, ownership checks |
   183	
   184	Typical loop:
   185	
   186	```sh
   187	S=.cursor/skills/verify-fs-billing/scripts
   188	RUN_ID=$($S/launch.sh | tail -n1)
   189	$S/doctor.sh
   190	$S/drive.mjs customer-login
   191	$S/cleanup.sh
   192	ls docs/evidence/verify-fs-billing/$RUN_ID/customer-login
   193	```
   194	
   195	Keep the map honest as the app grows: run `/maintain-verification-skill`.
     1	# FS-Billing verification map
     2	
     3	This directory is the maintained source for verifying FS-Billing's user-facing behavior. It covers the customer portal and admin shell in `apps/web`, the `/api` surface of `apps/api`, the auth CLIs, and the separate FreeSWITCH/SIPp e2e. Read this index before driving, then use the matching feature file as the recipe. `S=.cursor/skills/verify-fs-billing/scripts` throughout.
     4	
     5	## Baseline preconditions
     6	
     7	- A run launched by `$S/launch.sh`, with its own `postgres:17.9-alpine` container `fsb-verify-<RUN_ID>`, its own API and web ports, and an empty database that was migrated, seeded, and given credentials by `auth:seed`.
     8	- `$S/doctor.sh` prints `doctor: healthy` for that run. Never drive a stack the run did not start. Never touch `fs-billing-postgres-1` or `127.0.0.1:5432`.
     9	- Seed facts the journeys rely on (`packages/db/src/seed.ts`):
    10	  - ABC001 is a CNY tenant with balance `-35.20`.
    11	  - GLB009 is a USD tenant with balance `-15.02`.
    12	  - CDT018 is `disabled`.
    13	  - The admins are `wangmin@example.com` (王敏) and `lijun@example.com` (李俊).
    14	  - Every portal login is `<lowercase code>@example.com`, and every seeded account uses `SEED_AUTH_PASSWORD`.
    15	- No seeded account has a live lock in `auth.login_failures`. Every journey checks this and exits BLOCKED if one does.
    16	
    17	## Driving conventions
    18	
    19	- Browser actions go through `$S/drive.mjs <feature-id>`, a headless Playwright Chromium against the run's web origin. Use `--headed` to watch.
    20	- HTTP actions go through `$S/api.sh`, via the web origin's `/api` proxy with the web `Origin` header.
    21	- DB reads go through `$S/sql.sh "<sql>"`, as the owner role in the run's container. Repo CLIs go through `$S/with-env.sh <command>`.
    22	- Use ARIA roles and accessible names from `apps/web/src`, quoted exactly as in the feature files (Chinese copy included). Do not use CSS classes or positions.
    23	- Journeys create sessions and failure counters as they run. Each one leaves the accounts it used unlocked. Run features one at a time: all credential routes share a 30-per-minute-per-IP rate limit.
    24	- A fresh launch is the reset. If a run's state is unclear, clean it up and launch again. Do not hand-edit rows to restore a baseline.
    25	
    26	## Proof and skip reporting
    27	
    28	- Every step records the user action, the HTTP status and error `code` behind it, and the resulting screen as a `.png` and an `.aria.yml`.
    29	- Every state change is also checked in rows: `auth.session`, `auth.login_failures`, `auth.account`, `operation_logs`, `tenant_balances`. The query and its rows are saved to `.sql.txt`.
    30	- A DB edit that stands in for elapsed time or an operator action is saved as its own `.sql.txt` and named in the step (expiring `auth.session.expires_at`, deleting session rows).
    31	- Evidence goes to `docs/evidence/verify-fs-billing/<RUN_ID>/<feature-id>/`. `steps.md` and `result.json` give PASS, FAIL, or BLOCKED per step.
    32	- BLOCKED (exit 2) means a precondition was missing. Report it with the reason, and never as PASS. Do not report an entry point as verified through a different entry point.
    33	- Report the run's git revision from `doctor.sh`, and note any uncommitted paths.
    34	
    35	## Feature entry contract
    36	
    37	Each feature file starts with an H1 title and one paragraph describing the user-visible behavior. It then uses exactly four H2 sections, in this order:
    38	
    39	1. `Sub-features`: short IDs, one line per behavior. They match the step IDs in `drive.mjs` output.
    40	2. `How to get to it (user POV)`: every user entry point.
    41	3. `Driving it with <harness>`: starts with `Preconditions:`, then labeled bullets. Each bullet pairs a user action with the exact command and the observable result, including DB side effects.
    42	4. `Gotchas`: traps that can waste or invalidate a run.
    43	
    44	Keep implementation details out of the map. Name only user paths, stable handles, required state, commands, and observable proof.
    45	
    46	## Features
    47	
    48	- [customer-login](./customer-login.md): portal login, redirect when signed out, the balance on the home page, and the session row.
    49	- [admin-login](./admin-login.md): admin login into the admin shell. The account kind decides the zone, whichever login page was used.
    50	- [login-errors](./login-errors.md): one message for an unknown email and a wrong password, lockout after 5 failures with a countdown, emergency unlock, and the disabled account.
    51	- [session-expiry](./session-expiry.md): an expired or revoked session goes to login, then back to the original URL with its query.
    52	- [cross-tenant](./cross-tenant.md): tenant A cannot read B, customer and admin APIs are split, and the IDOR suite.
    53	- [logout](./logout.md): logout from the desktop menu and the mobile sheet removes the session row, and revisiting redirects to login.
    54	- [theme-and-mobile](./theme-and-mobile.md): light, dark, and system themes persist; the 375px layout; the account sheet and nav drawer.
    55	- [not-found](./not-found.md): the 404 page in the portal and admin zones, signed in and signed out, plus the API 404.
    56	- [freeswitch-sipp](./freeswitch-sipp.md): a separate surface. How to run the SIPp e2e, what PASS looks like, and why it is not isolated.
     1	# Admin login
     2	
     3	An internal admin signs in on `/admin/login` and lands in the admin shell at `/admin`: the `概览` page, the admin's name in the account menu, and the admin accent (`html[data-product=admin]`). Where you land depends on the account kind in the login response, not on the login page used. A customer who signs in on the admin login page lands in the portal, and an admin who signs in on the portal login page lands in the admin shell.
     4	
     5	## Sub-features
     6	
     7	- `entry-redirect`: a signed-out visit to `/admin` redirects to `/admin/login`, which shows `heading '登录管理后台'` and the hint `客户请使用客户门户登录`.
     8	- `admin-shell`: `wangmin@example.com` reaches `/admin` with `heading '概览'`, `button '账号菜单'` containing `王敏`, and the text `欢迎，王敏。…`. An `auth.session` row joined to `admins` exists.
     9	- `kind-decides-zone`: a customer logging in on `/admin/login` lands on `/`, and a later visit to `/admin` bounces to `/`.
    10	- `admin-via-portal-login`: an admin logging in on `/login` lands on `/admin`.
    11	
    12	## How to get to it (user POV)
    13	
    14	- Open `/admin/login` directly.
    15	- Open any `/admin…` URL while signed out. The app redirects to `/admin/login`.
    16	- Log in as an admin on the portal page `/login`. The app routes by account kind.
    17	
    18	## Driving it with Playwright (drive.mjs)
    19	
    20	Preconditions:
    21	
    22	- `$S/doctor.sh` is healthy.
    23	- `wangmin@example.com` and `abc001@example.com` are not locked.
    24	
    25	- **Whole journey.** Run `$S/drive.mjs admin-login`. Exit `0`, and `steps.md` lists the four sub-feature IDs as PASS.
    26	- **Signed-out redirect.** Open `/admin`. The URL becomes `/admin/login`, and the page shows `heading '登录管理后台'` and `客户请使用客户门户登录`. Evidence: `01-admin-login.png`, `.aria.yml`.
    27	- **Admin login.** On `/admin/login`, fill `登录邮箱` with `wangmin@example.com` and the seeded password, then click `登录`. The URL becomes `/admin`, and the page shows `heading '概览'`, `欢迎，王敏。`, `button '账号菜单'` with `王敏`, and `html[data-product=admin]`. Evidence: `02-admin-home.png`, `.aria.yml`. `02-admin-session.sql.txt` shows `wangmin@example.com | 王敏 | t`.
    28	- **Customer on the admin page.** In a fresh context, log in on `/admin/login` as `abc001@example.com`. The URL becomes `/` and `heading '首页'` is visible. Then `page.goto('/admin')` stays on `/`. Evidence: `03-customer-via-admin-login.png`.
    29	- **Admin on the portal page.** In a fresh context, log in on `/login` as `wangmin@example.com`. The URL becomes `/admin`.
    30	- **API view.** Run `$S/api.sh login wangmin@example.com adm` then `$S/api.sh GET /api/admin/context adm`. The response is 200 with `"identity":{"kind":"admin",…,"name":"王敏"}`.
    31	
    32	## Gotchas
    33	
    34	- `/admin/login` shows `登录管理后台` as the h1 on both desktop and mobile. The mobile layout also shows an `FS-Billing 管理后台` pill. Do not assert the portal copy here.
    35	- Routing by kind happens client-side after a 200 login. The API accepts a customer login on either page, so the API response alone never proves the zone. Assert the final URL.
    36	- Admin login creates a normal 7-day session, and nothing in this journey logs it out. Later journeys that count `wangmin` sessions must count before and after rather than expect zero.
     1	# Cross-tenant isolation
     2	
     3	A customer can read only their own tenant's data, and customer and admin APIs are kept apart. The portal API takes no tenant or account ID from the client: the tenant comes from the session. So customer A's cookie always returns A's data, and B's IDs in admin URLs are refused with 403. A customer cookie on admin endpoints and an admin cookie on portal endpoints both get `403 FORBIDDEN`, and no cookie gets `401 UNAUTHENTICATED`. The repo's IDOR suite enumerates every customer, authenticated, and session operation from the live OpenAPI and checks both defenses: explicit filters and RLS.
     4	
     5	## Sub-features
     6	
     7	- `own-tenant-only`: A (`abc001`) and B (`glb009`) each `GET /api/portal/context` and get only their own tenant id and balance: A `-35.20 CNY`, B `-15.02 USD`. The tenant ids match the DB.
     8	- `customer-to-admin`: A's cookie on `GET /api/admin/context` and on `POST /api/admin/portal-accounts/<B's id>/reset-password` gets `403 FORBIDDEN`. B's `auth.account.updated_at` is unchanged and `operation_logs` has 0 `reset_password` rows.
     9	- `admin-to-portal`: an admin cookie on `GET /api/portal/context` gets `403 FORBIDDEN`.
    10	- `anonymous`: no cookie gets `401 UNAUTHENTICATED` with `action: reauthenticate`.
    11	- `idor-suite`: `pnpm test:idor` reports `Tests 15 passed (15)`, run on a scratch `fs_billing_test` DB inside the run's container.
    12	
    13	## How to get to it (user POV)
    14	
    15	- A logged-in customer's browser calls `/api/portal/context` from the portal home. Any HTTP client holding their cookie can call any `/api/*` path.
    16	- A customer pastes an admin URL such as `/admin` into the browser. The UI bounces them to `/` (see [admin-login](./admin-login.md)), and this feature proves the API also refuses.
    17	- A developer runs `pnpm test:idor`, and CI runs it too.
    18	
    19	## Driving it with curl via api.sh (drive.mjs orchestrates)
    20	
    21	Preconditions:
    22	
    23	- `$S/doctor.sh` is healthy. `abc001`, `glb009`, and `wangmin` are not locked.
    24	- `operation_logs` has no `reset_password` rows. A fresh launch has none.
    25	
    26	- **Whole journey.** Run `$S/drive.mjs cross-tenant`. Exit `0`, five steps PASS. Transcripts are saved as `NN-*.http`.
    27	- **Two tenants.** Run `$S/api.sh login abc001@example.com a`, `$S/api.sh login glb009@example.com b`, `$S/api.sh GET /api/portal/context a`, and `$S/api.sh GET /api/portal/context b`. Jar `a` returns `"code":"ABC001"` and `"amount":"-35.20"`. Jar `b` returns `"code":"GLB009"` and `"amount":"-15.02"`. Neither body contains the other tenant. `05-tenant-ids.sql.txt` maps codes to ids and balances.
    28	- **Customer on admin API.** Run `$S/api.sh GET /api/admin/context a`. The response is `403` with `"code":"FORBIDDEN"`. Get B's portal account id with `$S/sql.sh "SELECT p.id FROM portal_accounts p JOIN tenants t ON t.id=p.tenant_id WHERE t.code='GLB009'"`, then run `$S/api.sh POST /api/admin/portal-accounts/<id>/reset-password a`. The response is `403 FORBIDDEN`. `08-no-reset-side-effect.sql.txt` shows the same `updated_at` as before and `reset_logs 0`.
    29	- **Admin on portal API.** Run `$S/api.sh login wangmin@example.com admin` then `$S/api.sh GET /api/portal/context admin`. The response is `403 FORBIDDEN`.
    30	- **Anonymous.** Run `$S/api.sh GET /api/portal/context`. The response is `401` with `"code":"UNAUTHENTICATED"` and `"action":"reauthenticate"`.
    31	- **IDOR suite.** Run `$S/with-env.sh pnpm test:idor`. The output contains `Tests  15 passed (15)` (`13-test-idor.cli.txt`). The suite creates and drops `fs_billing_test` in OUR container. Confirm with `$S/sql.sh "SELECT datname FROM pg_database"`: no `fs_billing_test` remains.
    32	- **Mutation proof (optional, slower).** Run `$S/with-env.sh pnpm --filter @fs-billing/api prove:idor docs/evidence/verify-fs-billing/<RUN_ID>/cross-tenant/prove-idor`. It must report that baseline-app and baseline-owner pass, that mutation-owner detects B's balance leak, and that mutation-app passes (`docs/phase-4-api.md`).
    33	
    34	## Gotchas
    35	
    36	- There is no portal endpoint that takes a tenant id, so "A reads B by changing an id" cannot be attempted on the portal API. The real attack surfaces are the admin endpoints that take ids (the reset-password routes) and session or cookie confusion. Do not invent a URL like `/api/portal/tenants/<B>`: it returns `404 NOT_FOUND`, which proves nothing.
    37	- `test:idor` and `prove:idor` load the root `.env`. Run them only through `with-env.sh`, or they drop and recreate `fs_billing_test` on the user's `:5432` server.
    38	- Do not run `test:idor` while another DB suite runs against the same container. It drops and recreates `fs_billing_test` every time.
    39	- POSTs need `Origin` set to a trusted origin, or they return `403 FORBIDDEN` before authorization runs. `api.sh` sends the web origin. A hand-written curl without it gives a misleading 403.
     1	# Customer login
     2	
     3	A customer signs in on the portal login page with their login email and password and lands on the portal home (`/`). The home page shows their tenant code and name in the top bar and the current balance in the tenant's currency. A signed-out visit to any portal page redirects to `/login`.
     4	
     5	## Sub-features
     6	
     7	- `entry-redirect`: a signed-out visit to `/` redirects to `/login`, which shows the `登录` form.
     8	- `portal-login`: valid credentials return 200 with `identity.kind = customer` and navigate to `/`.
     9	- `balance`: the home page shows the crumb `ABC001 · 深圳市某某科技有限公司` and the region `当前余额` with `-¥35.20` and `CNY`.
    10	- `db-side-effects`: an `auth.session` row with a 7-day TTL exists, and the DB balance equals the UI balance.
    11	- `usd-balance`: a USD tenant sees a dollar balance (`-$15.02`).
    12	
    13	## How to get to it (user POV)
    14	
    15	- Open the web origin's `/login` directly.
    16	- Open any portal URL (`/`, `/?tab=…`) while signed out. The app redirects to `/login`.
    17	- Use `/admin/login` with a customer account. See [admin-login](./admin-login.md) `kind-decides-zone`; it also ends on `/`.
    18	
    19	## Driving it with Playwright (drive.mjs)
    20	
    21	Preconditions:
    22	
    23	- `$S/doctor.sh` is healthy.
    24	- `abc001@example.com` and `glb009@example.com` are not locked. The journey checks this and exits 2 if either is locked.
    25	- The seed is unchanged: ABC001 has `-35.20` CNY and GLB009 has `-15.02` USD.
    26	
    27	- **Whole journey.** Run `$S/drive.mjs customer-login`. Exit `0` and every step shows PASS in `docs/evidence/verify-fs-billing/<RUN_ID>/customer-login/steps.md`.
    28	- **Signed-out redirect.** Open `/`. Step `entry-redirect`. The URL becomes `/login` and `heading '登录'` is visible. Evidence: `01-login-form.png`, `01-login-form.aria.yml`.
    29	- **Log in.** Fill `登录邮箱` with `abc001@example.com` and `密码` with the seeded password, then click `button '登录'`. Step `portal-login`. `http.log` shows `POST /api/auth/login -> 200` with `"kind":"customer"` and `"code":"ABC001"`, and the URL becomes `/`.
    30	- **Balance shown.** Step `balance`. The page shows `heading '首页'`, the crumb `ABC001 · 深圳市某某科技有限公司`, and `region '当前余额'` containing `-¥35.20` and `CNY`, with `html[data-product=portal]`. Evidence: `02-portal-home.png`, `02-portal-home.aria.yml`.
    31	- **Session row and DB balance.** Step `db-side-effects` reads them with `$S/sql.sh`. `03-session-and-balance.sql.txt` shows `abc001@example.com | t | 127.0.0.1 | -35.20`: a session expiring more than 6 days out, and `tenant_balances` equal to the UI value.
    32	- **USD tenant.** Log in as `glb009@example.com` in a fresh context. Step `usd-balance`. `region '当前余额'` contains `-$15.02`. Evidence: `04-usd-home.png`.
    33	- **API-only check.** Run `$S/api.sh login abc001@example.com a` then `$S/api.sh GET /api/portal/context a`. The response is 200 with `"balance":{"currency":"CNY","amount":"-35.20"}`.
    34	
    35	## Gotchas
    36	
    37	- The desktop form has the visible `heading '登录'`. Below 640px that h1 is hidden and `登录客户门户` shows instead, so use a desktop viewport for this journey.
    38	- The balance renders only after `/api/portal/context` resolves, and a skeleton shows until then. Assert on the region text, not on the skeleton being gone.
    39	- `-¥35.20` is red because the balance is negative. Any change to the seed balance changes the expected string. Fix the seed expectation in this file and in `drive.mjs` together, and never loosen it to a regex.
    40	- A successful login also deletes that email's `auth.login_failures` row. Logging in before [login-errors](./login-errors.md) can hide a counter you meant to inspect.
    41	- `bjh003@example.com` belongs to launch and doctor. Do not use it in journeys.
     1	# FreeSWITCH / SIPp e2e (separate surface)
     2	
     3	The telephony surface has no UI. A softswitch user places a call through FreeSWITCH. The billing Lua asks the Worker to authorize it (`maxSeconds`), FreeSWITCH bridges or rejects the call (402/403/484), and on hangup posts the CDR through the nginx relay to the Worker, which inserts `raw_cdrs`/`cdrs` and debits the ledger. `freeswitch/test/run-integration.sh` drives this end to end with real FreeSWITCH, SIPp, nginx, and Worker processes and an observer that captures INVITEs. The browser harness (`drive.mjs`) does not drive it, and `launch.sh` does not start it.
     4	
     5	## Sub-features
     6	
     7	- `number-cases`: six dialing formats (`+44`, `0044`, domestic mobile, domestic landline, `+1305`, bare `1305`). In each, the upstream INVITE keeps the original number. The authorize request has `callee == billingNumber` normalized and `dialedNumber` raw. The CDR `billing_number` is normalized.
     8	- `rejects`: a disabled tenant gets SIP 403, insufficient balance gets 402, and an invalid number gets 484 before any authorize request. None is bridged upstream.
     9	- `degrade`: when the Worker refuses or stalls, the call degrades per the Lua rules, and the observer records no authorize request.
    10	- `time-limit`: a real 60 s allotted call lasts 59–60 s. It is billed 1 minute, CDR amount `0.01`, ledger `-0.01`, balance `0.01 → 0`.
    11	- `cdr-replay`: with nginx down, the CDR lands in the retry dir. `replay-cdr.sh` re-posts it after nginx returns, and exactly one charge results.
    12	- `lua-unit`: `lua5.3 freeswitch/tests/billing_authorize_test.lua` (also run by `pnpm check`).
    13	
    14	## How to get to it (user POV)
    15	
    16	- CI: `.github/workflows/freeswitch.yml` job `e2e`, on push or PR touching `freeswitch/**`, `apps/worker/**`, or `packages/**`, nightly, or `workflow_dispatch`. It needs the private GHCR image built by the `image` job (`SIGNALWIRE_TOKEN`).
    17	- Locally: `bash freeswitch/test/run-integration.sh [log-dir]`, with the images `fs-billing-freeswitch:local` and the SIPp image built from `freeswitch/test/sipp.Dockerfile`. `--build` rebuilds FreeSWITCH and needs `SIGNALWIRE_TOKEN`.
    18	- A real test-bench call (phase 10) is a different acceptance, outside this skill.
    19	
    20	## Driving it with run-integration.sh
    21	
    22	Preconditions:
    23	
    24	- Linux with Docker host networking, Node, pnpm, Python 3, curl, and `docker images` showing `fs-billing-freeswitch:local` (or set `FS_IMAGE`).
    25	- Host ports `3001` (Worker), `3002` (nginx relay), `3003` (stall), `3004` (authorize observer), and `5060`/`5062` (SIP) are free. They are fixed and host-networked, so only one SIPp run per machine, and never alongside a dev Worker on 3001.
    26	- **Isolation.** By default the script runs `docker compose -f compose.yaml` against the user's compose project, which is `fs-billing-postgres-1` on `:5432`, migrates it, inserts `FSI…` test tenants, and stops it afterwards if it started it. Never run it bare here. Point it at a throwaway compose project instead:
    27	
    28	  ```sh
    29	  SIPP_RUN=fsb-sipp-$(date -u +%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ)
    30	  SIPP_PORT=$(python3 -c 'import socket; s=socket.socket(); s.bind(("127.0.0.1",0)); print(s.getsockname()[1])')
    31	  SIPP_PW=$(python3 -c 'import secrets; print(secrets.token_hex(16))')
    32	  SIPP_LOG=docs/evidence/verify-fs-billing/$SIPP_RUN/freeswitch-sipp
    33	  COMPOSE_PROJECT_NAME=$SIPP_RUN POSTGRES_PORT=$SIPP_PORT POSTGRES_PASSWORD=$SIPP_PW \
    34	  DATABASE_URL=postgresql://fs_billing:$SIPP_PW@127.0.0.1:$SIPP_PORT/fs_billing \
    35	  APP_DATABASE_URL=postgresql://fs_billing_app:$SIPP_PW-app@127.0.0.1:$SIPP_PORT/fs_billing \
    36	    bash freeswitch/test/run-integration.sh "$PWD/$SIPP_LOG"
    37	  docker compose -p "$SIPP_RUN" -f compose.yaml down -v   # remove ONLY the throwaway project + volume
    38	  ```
    39	
    40	  `docker compose config` confirms that these variables give project `fsb-sipp-…`, `127.0.0.1:$SIPP_PORT`, and volume `fsb-sipp-…_postgres_data`. That substitution was checked. The full SIPp run was **not** executed when this skill was generated.
    41	
    42	- **Run.** Execute the block above. The script prints a `用例	结果` table, and every row must read `PASS`. Exit code `0` means all cases passed. Any failure appends a `FAIL` row for the current case and exits non-zero.
    43	- **Read the proof.** In `$SIPP_LOG/`: `results.tsv` (PASS/FAIL per case), the observer's INVITE and authorize captures, `containers.log`, `cdr/`, `replay.retry.json` / `replay.archive.json`, and the SQL waits on `raw_cdrs`/`cdrs` keyed by controlled `sip_call_id`. For extra DB proof, query the throwaway DB with `docker compose -p "$SIPP_RUN" -f compose.yaml exec -T postgres psql -U fs_billing -d fs_billing -c "<sql>"` before running `down -v`.
    44	- **Lua unit only.** Run `lua5.3 freeswitch/tests/billing_authorize_test.lua`. Exit `0`.
    45	- **CI evidence.** A PR's `FreeSWITCH integration` workflow uploads the `freeswitch-integration` artifact (the same log dir). `docs/evidence/phase-4-api/ci.txt` records the last all-PASS run.
    46	
    47	## Gotchas
    48	
    49	- Running `run-integration.sh` with no overrides writes FSI tenants into the user's dev DB and may stop their Postgres container. Always use the throwaway compose project above, and remove it with `down -v` for that project name only.
    50	- `WORKER_SHARED_KEY` is generated per run when unset. Do not export a real key into the logs.
    51	- The time-limit case really waits about 60 s per call, so the whole run takes several minutes. Do not shorten it: billing at 61 s counts as 2 minutes and the assertions are exact.
    52	- If the image for the current `freeswitch/install/**` and `freeswitch/docker/**` hash is not built, CI skips the e2e with a notice. A skipped workflow is not a PASS.
    53	- `launch.sh`'s API and web stack is independent of this. It does not start the Worker, so `/authorize` and CDR ingest cannot be shown through the browser harness.
     1	# Login errors
     2	
     3	Login failures must not reveal whether an email exists. An unknown email and a wrong password produce the same status, code, message, and on-screen alert. After 5 failed attempts within 15 minutes, the account is locked for 15 minutes. The page then shows a countdown driven by `retryAfterSeconds` and disables the form, and the DB holds a counter row. An operator's emergency unlock CLI deletes the counter so the user can log in again. A disabled account gets its own message and no session.
     4	
     5	## Sub-features
     6	
     7	- `same-message`: an unknown email and a wrong password both return `401 INVALID_CREDENTIALS "账号或密码错误"`. The UI shows the alert `登录邮箱或密码错误` / `请检查后重新输入`. Both emails get an `auth.login_failures` row with `failed_count = 1`.
     8	- `clear-abc001`: a correct login deletes that email's counter. The unknown email's counter is removed with `auth:unlock`.
     9	- `lockout`: attempts 1–4 return `401 INVALID_CREDENTIALS` and attempt 5 returns `429 ACCOUNT_LOCKED` with `retryAfterSeconds ≈ 900`. The alert reads `登录失败次数过多，已暂时锁定` / `请在 15分00秒 后重试`. The submit button is disabled and named by the countdown, which ticks down. The email field is disabled. The DB row has `failed_count = 5` and `locked_until ≈ now + 15 min`.
    10	- `locked-correct-password`: the correct password while locked still returns `429 ACCOUNT_LOCKED`.
    11	- `emergency-unlock`: `auth:unlock` prints `解除锁定计数 1 行` and the counter row is gone. A login with the correct password then reaches `/`.
    12	- `disabled`: CDT018's portal login returns `403 ACCOUNT_DISABLED`. The alert reads `该账号已停用` / `如需恢复使用，请联系客服`, and `cdt018` has 0 `auth.session` rows.
    13	
    14	## How to get to it (user POV)
    15	
    16	- Submit the form on `/login` or `/admin/login` with a wrong password or an unknown email. Both pages share the logic. Admin copy differs only for disabled (`该管理员账号已停用`).
    17	- Repeat wrong passwords five times on one email.
    18	- An operator runs `pnpm --filter @fs-billing/api auth:unlock <email>`. In this skill that is always `$S/with-env.sh pnpm --filter @fs-billing/api auth:unlock <email>`.
    19	- Log in as an account whose tenant or profile is disabled.
    20	
    21	## Driving it with Playwright (drive.mjs)
    22	
    23	Preconditions:
    24	
    25	- `$S/doctor.sh` is healthy.
    26	- `hzx014@example.com` has no `auth.login_failures` row. Check with `$S/sql.sh "SELECT * FROM auth.login_failures WHERE email_key='hzx014@example.com'"`. The journey exits 2 if the row exists.
    27	- `abc001@example.com` is not locked. CDT018 is seeded `disabled`.
    28	- Fewer than about 15 credential requests from this machine in the last minute (shared limit of 30/min/IP).
    29	
    30	- **Whole journey.** Run `$S/drive.mjs login-errors`. Exit `0`, all six steps PASS.
    31	- **Unknown email vs wrong password.** On `/login`, submit `nobody-verify@example.com` / `wrong-password-verify`, then `abc001@example.com` / `wrong-password-verify`. Step `same-message`. Both responses are `401 INVALID_CREDENTIALS` with identical `message`, and `getByRole('alert')` has text `登录邮箱或密码错误请检查后重新输入` both times. Evidence: `01-unknown-email.png`, `02-wrong-password.png`, `http.log`. `03-counters-after-one-failure.sql.txt` shows both emails with `failed_count 1`.
    32	- **Clear counters.** Log in correctly as `abc001`, then run `$S/with-env.sh pnpm --filter @fs-billing/api auth:unlock nobody-verify@example.com`. Step `clear-abc001`. The `abc001` counter count is `0`. Evidence: `04-unlock-unknown.cli.txt`.
    33	- **Lock the account.** In a fresh page, submit `hzx014@example.com` with `wrong-password-1` through `wrong-password-5`. Step `lockout`. Attempts 1–4 return 401, attempt 5 returns 429 with `retryAfterSeconds` > 850. The alert contains `登录失败次数过多，已暂时锁定` and matches `请在 1[45]分\d{2}秒 后重试`. `button /^请在 \d+分\d{2}秒 后重试$/` is disabled and its name changes after 2 s (for example `15分00秒` to `14分58秒`). `登录邮箱` is disabled. Evidence: `05-locked.png`, `05-locked.aria.yml`. `06-lock-counter.sql.txt` shows `hzx014@example.com | 5 | t`.
    34	- **Correct password while locked.** Run `$S/api.sh login hzx014@example.com locked`. Step `locked-correct-password`. The response is `429 ACCOUNT_LOCKED`. Evidence: `07-locked-correct-password.http`.
    35	- **Emergency unlock.** Run `$S/with-env.sh pnpm --filter @fs-billing/api auth:unlock hzx014@example.com`. Step `emergency-unlock`. Output `解除锁定计数 1 行` (`08-auth-unlock.cli.txt`). `09-counter-after-unlock.sql.txt` shows `counter_rows 0`. After a reload and a correct login, the URL is `/` and the page shows `HZX014 · 杭州星讯通信有限公司` (`10-after-unlock.png`).
    36	- **Disabled account.** On `/login`, submit `cdt018@example.com` with the seeded password. Step `disabled`. The response is `403 ACCOUNT_DISABLED`, and the alert contains `该账号已停用` and `如需恢复使用，请联系客服` (`11-disabled.png`). `12-disabled-state.sql.txt` shows `CDT018 | disabled | 0` sessions.
    37	
    38	## Gotchas
    39	
    40	- The lock engages on the fifth failure itself (429 on attempt 5), not on a sixth try. A loop that expects five 401s fails.
    41	- The UI countdown is computed in the browser from `retryAfterSeconds` at response time. Assert that it changes, not an exact second, because the label can read `15分00秒` or `14分59秒`.
    42	- The `auth:unlock` CLI loads the root `.env`. It reaches our container only because `with-env.sh` exports `DATABASE_URL` first, and exported variables win over `.env`. Running it bare unlocks the user's dev DB instead.
    43	- Unknown emails also get counter rows: the API reserves before checking. Clean them up with `auth:unlock` so later runs start clean, as the journey does.
    44	- The API's 30/min/IP credential limit returns `429 RATE_LIMITED` with the alert `请求过于频繁`. That is a different alert from the lock. If you see it, wait a minute and rerun. Do not count it as lockout proof.
    45	- Stale counters expire after 15 minutes through a 1-minute background cleanup. A rerun soon after a failed run may be BLOCKED until you unlock.
    46	- No admin profile is seeded as disabled, so the admin copy `该管理员账号已停用` cannot be shown without changing data. The journey covers the portal copy only. Report the admin variant as not covered.
     1	# Logout
     2	
     3	A signed-in user logs out from the account menu: the avatar dropdown on desktop, or the `账号菜单` bottom sheet on mobile. The server deletes that session row and clears the cookie, and the app returns to the zone's login page. Revisiting a protected page afterwards redirects to login, and the old token no longer authenticates.
     4	
     5	## Sub-features
     6	
     7	- `desktop-menu`: `button '账号菜单'` opens a `menu` showing the account. Clicking `menuitem '退出登录'` returns to `/login`. The `auth.session` row for this browser's token goes from 1 to 0, and the cookie is gone.
     8	- `revisit`: `/` redirects to `/login`, and replaying the old token on `GET /api/auth/session` returns `401 UNAUTHENTICATED`.
     9	- `mobile-sheet`: at 375px, `button '账号菜单'` opens `dialog '账号菜单'`. Clicking `button '退出登录'` returns to `/login`, and live `abc001` sessions drop by exactly 1.
    10	
    11	## How to get to it (user POV)
    12	
    13	- Desktop (≥ 768px): click the account name (top right), then `退出登录`.
    14	- Mobile (< 768px): tap the avatar (top right), then `退出登录` in the bottom sheet.
    15	- The blocked page (disabled account mid-session) has its own `退出登录` button, which uses the same flow.
    16	
    17	## Driving it with Playwright (drive.mjs)
    18	
    19	Preconditions:
    20	
    21	- `$S/doctor.sh` is healthy. `abc001@example.com` is not locked.
    22	
    23	- **Whole journey.** Run `$S/drive.mjs logout`. Exit `0`, three steps PASS.
    24	- **Desktop logout.** Log in as `abc001`. The journey reads this browser's session token from the cookie and confirms `SELECT count(*) FROM auth.session WHERE token=<token>` is `1`. Click `button '账号菜单'`. `menu` contains `abc001@example.com` (`01-account-menu.png`, `.aria.yml`). Click `menuitem '退出登录'`. The URL becomes `/login` and the same query returns `0`. `02-session-row.sql.txt` records the before and after; the token itself is not written. No `better-auth.session_token` cookie remains (`03-after-logout.png`). `http.log` shows `POST /api/auth/logout -> 204`.
    25	- **Revisit.** Open `/`. The URL becomes `/login`. Send `GET /api/auth/session` with the old token as the cookie. The response is `401 {"code":"UNAUTHENTICATED"}`, logged in `http.log`.
    26	- **Mobile logout.** In a 375×812 touch context, log in as `abc001`. Count live sessions with `$S/sql.sh "SELECT count(*) FROM auth.session s JOIN auth.\"user\" u ON u.id=s.user_id WHERE u.email='abc001@example.com' AND s.expires_at > now()"`. Tap `button '账号菜单'`, then `button '退出登录'` inside `dialog '账号菜单'`. The URL becomes `/login` and the count drops by 1 (`04-mobile-after-logout.png`).
    27	- **API view.** Run `$S/api.sh login abc001@example.com l`, then `$S/api.sh POST /api/auth/logout l` returns `204` with `set-cookie: better-auth.session_token=; … Max-Age=0`. Running `$S/api.sh POST /api/auth/logout l` again returns `401`.
    28	
    29	## Gotchas
    30	
    31	- Logout deletes only this browser's session. Other sessions for the same user (other journeys, other contexts) stay live. Assert on this token, or on the count dropping by exactly one, never on "zero sessions for the user".
    32	- On desktop the menu item is a Radix `menuitem` inside `menu`. On mobile it is a plain `button` inside `dialog '账号菜单'`. The same selector does not work for both.
    33	- If the logout request fails for a reason other than 401, the UI stays signed in and shows `退出失败，请检查网络后重试`. If you see that, check API health before you retry.
    34	- Logout is excluded from the credential rate limit. It does not use up the 30/min budget.
     1	# Not found (404)
     2	
     3	Unknown URLs show a 404 page: the text `404`, `找不到这个页面`, an explanation, and a `返回首页` link to the zone's home. The zone comes from the path: `/admin/...` is the admin zone, everything else is the portal. Signed-out users, or users from the other zone, see a bare 404. Signed-in users in their own zone see it inside the shell. Unknown `/api/*` paths return a JSON `404 NOT_FOUND`.
     4	
     5	## Sub-features
     6	
     7	- `portal-signed-out`: `/no-such-page` shows `heading '找不到这个页面'` and `页面不存在或链接已失效，请检查地址是否正确`. `link '返回首页'` leads to `/` and then redirects to `/login`.
     8	- `admin-signed-out`: `/admin/no-such-page` shows the admin-zone 404 (`html[data-product=admin]`). `返回首页` leads to `/admin` and then redirects to `/admin/login`.
     9	- `admin-in-shell`: a signed-in admin on `/admin/no-such-page` sees the 404 inside the shell (`button '账号菜单'`, crumb `找不到页面`) with `返回首页即回到「概览」`. `返回首页` leads to `/admin`.
    10	- `portal-in-shell`: a signed-in customer on `/x/y` sees the 404 inside the portal shell, with the crumb `ABC001 · 深圳市某某科技有限公司`.
    11	- `api-404`: `GET /api/no-such-route` returns `404 {"error":{"code":"NOT_FOUND",…}}`.
    12	
    13	## How to get to it (user POV)
    14	
    15	- Type or follow a bad link under the portal (`/anything`) or the admin area (`/admin/anything`), signed in or out.
    16	- An HTTP client calls a non-existent `/api/...` path.
    17	
    18	## Driving it with Playwright (drive.mjs)
    19	
    20	Preconditions:
    21	
    22	- `$S/doctor.sh` is healthy. `abc001` and `wangmin` are not locked.
    23	
    24	- **Whole journey.** Run `$S/drive.mjs not-found`. Exit `0`, five steps PASS.
    25	- **Portal, signed out.** Open `/no-such-page`. The page shows `heading '找不到这个页面'` and the explanation (`01-portal-404.png`, `.aria.yml`). Click `link '返回首页'`. The URL becomes `/login`.
    26	- **Admin, signed out.** Open `/admin/no-such-page`. The page shows the heading and `html[data-product=admin]` (`02-admin-404-signed-out.png`). Click `返回首页`. The URL becomes `/admin/login`.
    27	- **Admin, in shell.** Log in as `wangmin` on `/admin/login`, then open `/admin/no-such-page`. The page shows the heading, `button '账号菜单'`, and `返回首页即回到「概览」` (`03-admin-404-shell.png`, `.aria.yml`). Click `返回首页`. The URL becomes `/admin`.
    28	- **Portal, in shell.** Log in as `abc001`, then open `/x/y`. The page shows the heading and the crumb `ABC001 · 深圳市某某科技有限公司` (`04-portal-404-shell.png`).
    29	- **API 404.** Run `$S/api.sh GET /api/no-such-route`. The response is `404` with `"code":"NOT_FOUND"` (`05-api-404.http`).
    30	
    31	## Gotchas
    32	
    33	- The bare 404 and the in-shell 404 depend on whether the session's identity matches the path's zone. A customer on `/admin/nope` gets the bare admin 404, not the portal shell.
    34	- The page renders nothing while the session query is pending. Wait for the heading, not for `load`.
    35	- `vite preview` serves `index.html` for every unknown path (SPA fallback), so the HTTP status of a page 404 is 200. Only `/api/*` gives a real 404 status. Do not assert a 404 status on page URLs.
    36	- `返回首页即回到「概览」` is hidden below 768px. Use a desktop viewport for `admin-in-shell`.
     1	# Session expiry and return-to
     2	
     3	When a session expires or is revoked, the user is sent to the login page of their zone. After logging in again, they return to the exact page they were on, query string included. There are two cases. Navigating with an expired session redirects to `/login?reason=expired`, which shows a `登录已过期` banner. A revoked session discovered while on a page opens a blocking dialog, whose `重新登录` button leads to `/admin/login?reason=expired`. A remembered path from the other zone is ignored after login, and the user lands on their own home.
     4	
     5	## Sub-features
     6	
     7	- `expired-redirect`: with an expired `auth.session.expires_at`, opening `/?tab=ledger&type=%E5%85%85%E5%80%BC` leads to `/login?reason=expired` with the status banner `登录已过期，请重新登录` / `登录后将返回之前的页面`. Logging in returns to the same URL.
     8	- `revoked-dialog`: deleting the admin's session rows while they are on `/admin?view=verify`, then a refocus refetch, opens `dialog '登录已过期，请重新登录'` containing `登录后将回到当前页面`. Clicking `重新登录` goes to `/admin/login?reason=expired`. Logging in returns to `/admin?view=verify`.
     9	- `cross-zone-fallback`: a signed-out visit to `/admin?from=portal-user`, then a customer login on `/admin/login`, lands on `/`.
    10	
    11	## How to get to it (user POV)
    12	
    13	- Leave a tab past the 7-day session lifetime, then navigate or reload.
    14	- An admin resets your password, or `auth:bootstrap --replace` runs, while you have the app open. Either deletes your sessions. Return to the tab.
    15	- Follow a deep link (bookmark or shared URL) while signed out.
    16	
    17	## Driving it with Playwright (drive.mjs)
    18	
    19	Preconditions:
    20	
    21	- `$S/doctor.sh` is healthy. `abc001@example.com` and `lijun@example.com` are not locked.
    22	
    23	- **Whole journey.** Run `$S/drive.mjs session-expiry`. Exit `0`, three steps PASS.
    24	- **Expire and deep-link.** Log in as `abc001`. Expire the session in the DB, standing in for 7 days passing: `$S/sql.sh "UPDATE auth.session SET expires_at = now() - interval '1 minute' WHERE user_id = (SELECT id FROM auth.\"user\" WHERE email='abc001@example.com')"` (saved as `01-expire-sessions.sql.txt`). Open `/?tab=ledger&type=%E5%85%85%E5%80%BC`. The URL becomes `/login?reason=expired` and `getByRole('status')` contains both banner lines (`02-expired-banner.png`, `.aria.yml`). Log in again. The URL is exactly `/?tab=ledger&type=%E5%85%85%E5%80%BC` and `heading '首页'` is visible (`03-returned.png`).
    25	- **Revoke while on a page.** Log in on `/admin/login` as `lijun@example.com` and open `/admin?view=verify`. Delete the sessions, as an admin reset does: `$S/sql.sh "DELETE FROM auth.session WHERE user_id = (SELECT id FROM auth.\"user\" WHERE email='lijun@example.com')"` (saved as `04-revoke-sessions.sql.txt`). Dispatch `visibilitychange` on `document`. `dialog '登录已过期，请重新登录'` appears with `登录后将回到当前页面` (`05-expired-dialog.png`, `.aria.yml`). Click `button '重新登录'`. The URL is `/admin/login?reason=expired`. Log in. The URL is `/admin?view=verify` (`06-returned.png`).
    26	- **Wrong-zone return path.** In a fresh context, open `/admin?from=portal-user`. The URL becomes `/admin/login`. Log in as `abc001`. The URL is `/`.
    27	- **API view.** After the expiry update, `$S/api.sh GET /api/auth/session <jar>` returns `401 SESSION_EXPIRED` with `"action":"reauthenticate"`. After the delete, it returns `401 UNAUTHENTICATED`.
    28	
    29	## Gotchas
    30	
    31	- The two cases differ by server code. `SESSION_EXPIRED` (row exists, past `expires_at`) adds `?reason=expired` and the banner. `UNAUTHENTICATED` (row gone, or cookie cleared) redirects to plain `/login` with no banner. Clearing browser cookies does not prove the expired banner.
    32	- The dialog appears only when an in-page query refetches. Headless Chromium cannot really background a tab, so the journey dispatches `visibilitychange`, which is React Query's refocus trigger. This is the one synthetic event in the skill. Say so when you report.
    33	- The expiry and revocation are DB edits that stand in for time passing and for an admin action. Each one is saved as its own `.sql.txt`. Do not treat them as product behavior.
    34	- The return path lives in `sessionStorage`, so it is per tab. A login in a new tab or context goes home instead.
    35	- Session lifetime is 7 days with no sliding refresh visible to the user. Do not wait for a real expiry.
     1	# Theme and mobile layout
     2	
     3	Users pick a light, dark, or follow-system theme. The choice applies at once, persists in `localStorage` across reloads, and in system mode tracks the OS color scheme live. On a 375px phone, the shell switches to a mobile header with a `☰` navigation drawer and an avatar that opens a bottom sheet. The sheet shows the account, a segmented theme picker, and logout, and the content fits without horizontal scrolling.
     4	
     5	## Sub-features
     6	
     7	- `desktop-dark`: `button '主题'` (◐) opens `menuitemradio '深色'`. Choosing it sets `html[data-theme=dark]` and `localStorage['fs-billing.theme'] = 'dark'`, and the theme is still dark after a reload.
     8	- `desktop-light`: `menuitemradio '浅色'` sets `light` and stores `light`.
     9	- `system`: `menuitemradio '跟随系统'` stores `system`, and `data-theme` follows `prefers-color-scheme` dark and light live, without a reload.
    10	- `mobile-layout`: at 375×812 the portal home shows `region '当前余额'` with `-¥35.20` and `button '打开导航'`, and `scrollWidth` ≤ 375.
    11	- `account-sheet`: `button '账号菜单'` opens `dialog '账号菜单'` with `abc001@example.com`, `深圳市某某科技有限公司 · ABC001`, and `radiogroup '主题'`. Choosing `深色` makes `radio '深色'` checked and the theme dark.
    12	- `nav-drawer`: `button '打开导航'` opens `dialog '导航'`, whose `link '首页'` has `aria-current=page`.
    13	
    14	## How to get to it (user POV)
    15	
    16	- Desktop, signed in: the ◐ button in the top bar.
    17	- Mobile, signed in: avatar, then the `主题` segmented control in the account sheet.
    18	- The OS appearance setting, when the preference is `跟随系统` (the default, with nothing stored).
    19	- Any page at a narrow viewport (phone, or a narrow window under 768px).
    20	
    21	## Driving it with Playwright (drive.mjs)
    22	
    23	Preconditions:
    24	
    25	- `$S/doctor.sh` is healthy. `abc001@example.com` is not locked.
    26	- The browser context starts with `colorScheme: 'light'` and empty `localStorage`. Each journey context is new.
    27	
    28	- **Whole journey.** Run `$S/drive.mjs theme-and-mobile`. Exit `0`, six steps PASS.
    29	- **Dark and persistence.** Log in as `abc001`. Click `button '主题'`; `menu` shows three radios (`01-theme-menu.aria.yml`). Click `menuitemradio '深色'`. The page has `html[data-theme=dark]` and storage `dark`. Reload: the theme is still `dark` (`02-dark-after-reload.png`).
    30	- **Light.** Use `主题`, then `浅色`. The page has `data-theme=light` and storage `light`.
    31	- **System.** Use `主题`, then `跟随系统`. Storage is `system`. `page.emulateMedia({ colorScheme: 'dark' })` sets `data-theme=dark`, and `'light'` sets `light`, with no reload.
    32	- **Mobile layout.** In a new 375×812 `isMobile` context, log in. The URL is `/`. The page shows `region '当前余额'` with `-¥35.20` and `button '打开导航'`, and `document.documentElement.scrollWidth` ≤ 375 (`03-mobile-home.png`, `.aria.yml`).
    33	- **Account sheet.** Tap `button '账号菜单'`. `dialog '账号菜单'` shows the email, the tenant line, and `radiogroup '主题'` (`04-account-sheet.aria.yml`). Tap `深色`. The page has `data-theme=dark` and `radio '深色'` checked (`04-account-sheet-dark.png`). Press Escape.
    34	- **Nav drawer.** Tap `button '打开导航'`. `dialog '导航'` has `link '首页'` with `aria-current="page"` (`05-nav-drawer.png`).
    35	
    36	## Gotchas
    37	
    38	- The theme is applied before React renders, by an inline script in `index.html`, using the same `fs-billing.theme` key. A bad stored value falls back to `system`. Assert `data-theme`, not CSS colors.
    39	- The segmented control uses visually hidden native radios. Click the label text (`getByText('深色')` inside the radiogroup) and assert `getByRole('radio', { name: '深色' })` is checked.
    40	- The breakpoint is `md` (768px). At 640–767px the login page is in desktop layout but the shell is mobile. Use exactly 375px for "mobile" proof.
    41	- The theme preference is per browser (localStorage), not per account. A new context resets it, so persistence must be shown with a reload in the same context.
    42	- The theme menu exists only in the signed-in shell. The login pages have no theme control; they follow the stored or system theme.

    14	# Resolve the run: $VERIFY_RUN_ID, else the run launch.sh recorded last.
    15	verify_run_id() {
    16	  if [[ -n ${VERIFY_RUN_ID:-} ]]; then printf '%s\n' "$VERIFY_RUN_ID"; return; fi
    17	  [[ -f $VERIFY_STATE_ROOT/current ]] || die "no run recorded; run launch.sh or set VERIFY_RUN_ID"

    19	}
    20	
    21	# Load a run's state into the environment: RUN_ID, STATE, EVIDENCE, and run.env.
    22	verify_load() {
    23	  RUN_ID=$(verify_run_id)
    24	  [[ $RUN_ID =~ ^[0-9]{8}T[0-9]{6}Z-[0-9a-f]{6}$ ]] || die "malformed run id: $RUN_ID"
    25	  STATE=$VERIFY_STATE_ROOT/$RUN_ID
    26	  EVIDENCE=$VERIFY_EVIDENCE_ROOT/$RUN_ID
    27	  [[ -f $STATE/run.env ]] || die "no state for run $RUN_ID at $STATE"
    28	  set -a
    29	  # shellcheck disable=SC1091
    30	  source "$STATE/run.env"
    31	  set +a
    32	  export RUN_ID STATE EVIDENCE
    33	}

    35	# Replace every secret value from run.env with [REDACTED], plus cookie values.
    36	verify_redact() {
    37	  local args=() key value
    38	  for key in BETTER_AUTH_SECRET SEED_AUTH_PASSWORD PG_OWNER_PASSWORD PG_APP_PASSWORD PG_AUTH_PASSWORD; do
    39	    value=${!key:-}
    40	    [[ -n $value ]] && args+=(-e "s|${value}|[REDACTED]|g")
    41	  done
    42	  args+=(-e 's/(session_token=)[^;[:space:]"]+/\1[REDACTED]/g')
    43	  sed -E "${args[@]}"
    44	}

    46	verify_free_port() {
    47	  python3 -c 'import socket; s=socket.socket(); s.bind(("127.0.0.1",0)); print(s.getsockname()[1]); s.close()'
    48	}

    50	# True when pid is alive and carries this run's VERIFY_RUN_ID in its environment.
    51	verify_owns_pid() {
    52	  local pid=$1
    53	  [[ -r /proc/$pid/environ ]] || return 1
    54	  tr '\0' '\n' < "/proc/$pid/environ" | grep -qx "VERIFY_RUN_ID=$RUN_ID"
    55	}

    58	verify_port_pids() {
    59	  ss -Hltnp "sport = :$1" 2>/dev/null | grep -o 'pid=[0-9]*' | cut -d= -f2 | sort -u
    60	}

    63	  try {
    64	    body = JSON.parse(bodyText);
    65	  } catch {}
    66	  return { status, body };
    67	}
    68	/** Repo command through with-env.sh (run env wins over the root .env); output is redacted. */
    69	function cli(name, args) {
    70	  let result;
    71	  try {
    72	    result = execFileSync(join(scripts, 'with-env.sh'), args, { encoding: 'utf8', env: { ...process.env, VERIFY_RUN_ID: runId } });
    73	  } catch (error) {
    74	    write(`${name}.cli.txt`, `$ ${args.join(' ')}\n${error.stdout ?? ''}`);
    75	    throw new Error(`${args.join(' ')} exited ${error.status}`);
    76	  }
    77	  write(`${name}.cli.txt`, `$ ${args.join(' ')}\n${result}`);
    78	  return result;
    79	}
    80	async function step(id, action, fn) {
    81	  const started = Date.now();
    82	  try {
    83	    const detail = await fn();
    84	    steps.push({ id, action, result: 'PASS', detail: detail ?? '', ms: Date.now() - started });
    85	  } catch (error) {
    86	    steps.push({ id, action, result: error instanceof Precondition ? 'BLOCKED' : 'FAIL', detail: String(error?.message ?? error).split('\n').slice(0, 6).join(' / '), ms: Date.now() - started });
    87	    throw error;
    88	  }
    89	}
    90	
    91	let browser;
    92	async function newPage(options = {}) {
    93	  const context = await browser.newContext({ baseURL: web, locale: 'zh-CN', timezoneId: 'Asia/Shanghai', ...options });
    94	  const page = await context.newPage();
    95	  page.on('response', async (response) => {
    96	    const url = new URL(response.url());
    97	    if (!url.pathname.startsWith('/api/')) return;
    98	    let body = '';
    99	    try {
   100	      body = await response.text();
   101	    } catch {}
   102	    const sent = response.request().postData();
   103	    const shown = sent ? sent.replace(/("password"\s*:\s*")[^"]*"/, '$1[REDACTED]"') : '';
   104	    appendFileSync(join(out, 'http.log'), redact(`${new Date().toISOString()} ${response.request().method()} ${url.pathname}${url.search} ${shown} -> ${response.status()} ${body}\n`));
   105	  });
   106	  return page;
   107	}
   108	async function shot(page, name) {
   109	  await page.screenshot({ path: join(out, `${name}.png`), fullPage: true });
   110	}
   111	async function aria(page, name, locator = page.locator('body')) {
   112	  write(`${name}.aria.yml`, await locator.ariaSnapshot());
   113	}
   114	function path(page) {
   115	  const url = new URL(page.url());
   116	  return `${url.pathname}${url.search}`;
   117	}
   118	async function expectPath(page, expected) {
   119	  await expect.poll(() => path(page), { timeout: 10_000 }).toBe(expected);
   120	}
   121	/** Fill and submit the login form; resolves with the /api/auth/login response. */
   122	async function submitLogin(page, email, secret = password) {
   123	  await page.getByLabel('登录邮箱').fill(email);
   124	  await page.getByLabel('密码', { exact: true }).fill(secret);
   125	  const response = page.waitForResponse((r) => new URL(r.url()).pathname === '/api/auth/login' && r.request().method() === 'POST');
   126	  await page.getByRole('button', { name: '登录', exact: true }).click();
   127	  return response;
   128	}
   129	async function loginAs(page, loginPath, email) {
   130	  await page.goto(loginPath);
   131	  const response = await submitLogin(page, email);
   132	  if (response.status() !== 200) throw new Error(`login ${email} -> ${response.status()} ${await response.text()}`);
   133	}
   134	const sessionsOf = (email) => `SELECT count(*) FROM auth.session s JOIN auth."user" u ON u.id = s.user_id WHERE u.email = '${email}' AND s.expires_at > now()`;
   135	function requireUnlocked(email) {
   136	  const row = scalar(`SELECT coalesce(max(locked_until)::text, '') FROM auth.login_failures WHERE email_key = '${email}' AND locked_until > now()`);
   137	  if (row) throw new Precondition(`${email} is locked until ${row}; run auth:unlock (see login-errors.md) or relaunch`);
   138	}
   139	
   140	const journeys = {

   174	      expect(result).toMatch(/abc001@example\.com \| t\s+\|.*\| -35\.20/);

   181	      await shot(usd, '04-usd-home');

   183	    });
   184	  },

   210	    });
